<script>window.ShopifyAnalytics.merchantGoogleAnalytics = function() {
  var trackanalyticswithShopify = 0;
if (window.location.href.includes('checkouts') == true)  { 
trackanalyticswithShopify = 1;
checkoutpage = 1;
} 
else
{
checkoutpage = 0;
}
if (checkoutpage == 1) {
if (window.location.href.includes('thank_you') == true)   { trackanalyticswithShopify = 0;}

}

};

<script>
    var authChecker= document.getElementById('AuthState').contentWindow;

    function logOut() {
      authChecker.postMessage({action: 'logout'}, 'https://app.nixplay.com');
    }

    function displayChange(className, state){
      var displayElement = document.querySelectorAll('.'+className);
      for (var displayIndex = 0; displayIndex < displayElement.length; displayIndex++) {
      var element = displayElement[displayIndex];
      element.style.display= state;
      }
    }
    function addLogout(){
      var displayElement = document.querySelectorAll('.logout');
      for (var displayIndex = 0; displayIndex < displayElement.length; displayIndex++) {
      var element = displayElement[displayIndex];
      element.innerHTML= '<a class="account-nav-content-link auth-avatar" id="auth-avatar" href="https://app.nixplay.com"></a> <a class="account-nav-content-link logoutBtn" id="logoutBtn" href="#" >' + "Log out"+ '</a>';
      }

      logOutBtn = document.querySelectorAll('.logoutBtn');
      for (var logoutBtnIndex = 0; logoutBtnIndex < logOutBtn.length; logoutBtnIndex++) {
        var element = logOutBtn[logoutBtnIndex];
        element.addEventListener('click', function(){  logOut(); });
      }
    }
    function UpdateUserName(name){
      var displayElement = document.querySelectorAll('.auth-avatar');
      for (var displayIndex = 0; displayIndex < displayElement.length; displayIndex++) {
      var element = displayElement[displayIndex];
        element.innerText = name;
      }
    }
    function receiveMessage(event) {
      if(event.origin)
      var message = event.data
      if (message && event.origin) {
          if (message.state === 'logged') {
            var name = message.name
            if(!name || name.length == 0) {
              name = "My Account"
            }
            addLogout()
            displayChange('login', "none");
            displayChange('logout', "block");
            UpdateUserName(name)
          } else if (message.state === 'logged-out') {
            displayChange('login', "block");
            displayChange('logout', "none");
          } else if (message.state === 'redirect') {
            window.location.href = message.url
          }
      }

      // ...
    }
    window.addEventListener("message", receiveMessage, false);

    

    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
      authChecker.postMessage({action: 'check'}, '*');
    });

<script type="text/javascript">
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
(function($){

function priceFromCents(cents) {
    if (typeof(cents) != "number")
        return;

    return (cents / 100);
}

var deviceType = /iPad/.test(navigator.userAgent) ? "t" : /Mobile|iP(hone|od)|Android|BlackBerry|IEMobile|Silk/.test(navigator.userAgent) ? "m" : "d";
window.criteo_q = window.criteo_q || [];
window.criteo_q.push(
  { event: "setAccount", account: 53485 },
  { event: "setEmail", email: "" },
  { event: "setSiteType", type: deviceType },
  { event: "viewHome" }
  
);

function isRelevantCartUpdate(settings) {
    function dataContains(term) {
        return settings.data && settings.data.indexOf && settings.data.indexOf(term) > -1;
    }

    return (settings.url == "/cart/add.js" || settings.url == "/cart/update.js")
      && (
        dataContains('attributes[prod') ||
        (dataContains('id=') && dataContains('quantity='))
      );
}
$(document).ajaxSuccess(function(event, xhr, settings) {
    if (isRelevantCartUpdate(settings)) {
        $.getJSON("/cart.js", function(data) {
            var basketData = [];
            var item;

            if (!data.items || !data.items.length)
                return;

            for (var i = 0; i < data.items.length; i++) {
                item = data.items[i];

                basketData.push({
                    id: "shopify_DE_" + item.product_id + "_" + item.variant_id,
                    price: priceFromCents(item.price),
                    quantity: item.quantity
                });
            }

            window.criteo_q.push({
                event: "viewBasket",
                product: basketData
            });
        });
    }
});
})(window.$);
});
</script>

<script>
window.console.log(saleCollections);
(function(){
  'use strict';
  var productList = [];

  function addToProductList(prod) {
    var indx = -1;
    productList.forEach((obj, index) => {
      if(obj.sku === prod.sku) {
        indx = index;
      }
    });
    if(indx === -1 || productList.length === 0) {
      productList.push(prod);
    }
  }
  saleCollections.forEach((element) => {
    element.products.forEach((product) => {
      addToProductList({ productId: product.productId, saleSticker: element.saleSticker, saleCopy: element.saleCopy, saleColor: element.saleColor, saleImage: element.saleImage, saleUpTo: element.saleUpTo, saleAmount: element.saleAmount, productCollection: product.productCollection, productImage: product.productImage, productTitle: product.productTitle, productPrice: product.productPrice, productUrl: product.productUrl, productTags: product.productTags, productVariantId: product.productVariantId, productVariantOption: product.productVariantOption, sku: product.sku, productAvailable: product.productAvailable });
    });
  });

    if(element.productId)

      el.classList.remove('hide');

    strickerImage.forEach((el) => {
      el.dataset.lazyImage = element.saleSticker;
      el.classList.remove('hide');
    });
  });

      if (productList.length > 0) {

        var digitalElement = document.getElementById('digital-products-section');

  function formatMoney(val){
    if(!val) { return '00'}
    return window.theme.moneyFormat.replace('{amount}}', Number(val.toFixed(2)).toLocaleString('en-US')).replace('{','');
  }

  function updatePrice(line, quantity, targets, parent){
    targets.forEach(function(target){
      var newPrice;
      var sku = target.dataset.activeSku;
      var price = line.productPrice/100;
      var qtt = Number(quantity);
      if(line.sku == sku) {
        var discoutVal = 0;
        if(line.saleUpTo){
          if(Number(line.saleAmount) == 25) {
            if (qtt == 1 ) {
              discoutVal = 15/100;
            } else if (qtt == 2 ) {
              discoutVal = 20/100;
            } else{
              discoutVal = 25/100;
            }
          } else {
            discoutVal = line.saleAmount/100;
          }
        } else {
          discoutVal = line.saleAmount/100;
          
        }
        newPrice = price * (1-discoutVal)* qtt
        if(parent){
          parent.dataset.price = newPrice;
        }
        
        target.innerHTML = `
          <span class="text sale-price" style="color: ${line.saleColor}"><b> ${formatMoney(newPrice)}</b></span><small class="actual-price text light"><s> ${formatMoney(price*qtt)} </s></small>
          <div class="you-save" style="color: ${line.saleColor}"><small>${offer.you_save} ${formatMoney(price * discoutVal * qtt)} </small></div>
        `;

      }
    })
  }

  productList.forEach(function(line){
    var targets = document.querySelectorAll(`.sale-price-container-${line.sku}, .sale-price-container-${line.productId}`);
    targets.forEach(function(target){
      var qttEl = target.querySelector(`.quantity-${line.productId}`);
      var priceEl = target.querySelectorAll(`.product-price-${line.productId}`);
      var lastSku = '';
      if (qttEl) {
        qttEl.addEventListener('mouseup', function(){
          updatePrice(line, qttEl.value, priceEl, target)
        });
        qttEl.addEventListener('keyup', function(){
          updatePrice(line, qttEl.value, priceEl, target)
        });
        qttEl.addEventListener('change', function(){
          updatePrice(line, qttEl.value, priceEl, target)
        });
      }

      function checkOften(){
        requestAnimationFrame(checkOften);
        if(lastSku != priceEl[0].dataset.activeSku) {
          var qtt = (qttEl) ? qttEl.value : 1;
          updatePrice(line, qtt, priceEl, target);
          lastSku = priceEl[0].dataset.activeSku;
        }
      }
      if(priceEl[0]) {
        lastSku = priceEl[0].dataset.activeSku;
        checkOften();
      }
      updatePrice(line, 1, priceEl, target)


    })

  });




})();

(function() {
    var referralPeriod = 365;
    var url = window.location.href;
    var cjEvent;

     var start = url.indexOf('cjevent');

     var query = url.substring(start,end);
     var newEnd = query.indexOf('&');

     cjEvent = query.substring(8,newEnd);
     console.log('found cjevent in URL: ' + cjEvent);
     checkForCjCookie();
    } else {
     checkForCjCookie();
    };
    function checkForCjCookie() {
     var name = "cjevent=";
     var decodedCookie = decodeURIComponent(document.cookie);
     var ca = decodedCookie.split(';');
  
       for(var i=0; i<ca.length; i++) {
     var c = ca[i];
     while (c.charAt(0) == ' ') {
     c = c.substring(1);
     }
  
    if (c.indexOf(name) == 0) {
     var value = (c.substring(name.length));
     if (cjEvent != undefined) {
     console.log('Found CJ Cookie');
     isCjCookiePresent = true;
     updateCookie();
     } else if (value != undefined){
     console.log('Found CJ Cookie, even though nothing in URL');
     setCjCookie('cjevent', value, referralPeriod);
     } else { // console.log(i);
     }
    }
     }
     if (cjEvent && !isCjCookiePresent) {
     console.log('cjevent in URL but no cookie');
     setCjCookie('cjevent', cjEvent, referralPeriod);
     }
    }
    function setCjCookie(cname, cvalue, exdays) {
     var d = new Date();
     d.setTime(d.getTime() + (exdays*24*60*60*1000));
     var expires = "expires="+ d.toUTCString();
     document.cookie = cname + "=" + cvalue + ";" + expires + ";path=/";
     console.log('Cj Cookie Set')
    }
  
  
  
    function updateCookie(){
    if (isCjCookiePresent) {
    console.log('Deleting cookie');
    setCjCookie('cjevent',"",-1);
    console.log('Adding new cookie');
    setCjCookie('cjevent', cjEvent, referralPeriod);
    }
    }
  })();
